# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 027 DOCTRINE RESEARCH RUNTIME
# ============================================================================
# Global minor repeatable doctrine batches. Country rows are append-only and
# receipts are the transaction authority. Ordinary and Chaos Warfare actions
# are explicit static adapters; unknown custom rows are never executable.
# Built-in empty-track mastery uses native set_sub_doctrine followed by the
# exact one-point/readback transaction in the mastery adapter file.
# ============================================================================

doctrine_research_add_adapter_row = {
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_domain_ids value = doctrine_research_registry_domain }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_dispatch_ids value = doctrine_research_registry_dispatch }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_contract_versions value = doctrine_research_registry_contract_version }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_enabled value = doctrine_research_registry_enabled }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable value = doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_folder_ids value = doctrine_research_registry_folder }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_feature_gates value = doctrine_research_registry_feature_gate }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_ai_selectors value = doctrine_research_registry_ai_selector }
	add_to_array = { array = global.doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selectors value = doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selector }
}

doctrine_research_initialize_adapter_registry = {
	if = { limit = { OR = { NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_adapter_registry_initialized } NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_adapter_registry_version } } }
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_domain_ids
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_dispatch_ids
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_contract_versions
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_enabled
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_folder_ids
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_feature_gates
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_ai_selectors
		clear_array = global.doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selectors

		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable = 1 }

		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selector = 1 }

		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_feature_gate = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_ai_selector = 3 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selector = 3 }
		doctrine_research_add_adapter_row = yes
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_dispatch = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_contract_version = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_enabled = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_folder = constant:doctrine_research_folder.special_forces }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_feature_gate = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_ai_selector = 4 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selector = 4 }
		doctrine_research_add_adapter_row = yes
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_dispatch = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_contract_version = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_enabled = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_one_step_capable = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_folder = constant:doctrine_research_folder.land }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_feature_gate = 2 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_ai_selector = 5 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_cleanup_selector = 5 }
		doctrine_research_add_adapter_row = yes
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.future_custom }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_registry_dispatch = constant:doctrine_research_domain.future_custom }

		doctrine_research_add_adapter_row = yes
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_adapter_registry_initialized
	}
}

# Global scope. Event 027 stores the last accepted evolution stage separately
# from the Chaos Meter. The first firing may use the highest stage already
# eligible in the campaign; later promotions are one enabled stage at a time
# after the centralized pacing interval has elapsed.
doctrine_research_get_highest_enabled_stage = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_target_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_i_enabled = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_target_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.gathering_storm } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_ii_enabled = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_target_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.rising_chaos } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_iii_enabled = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_target_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.chaos_tier } }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_iv_enabled = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_target_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.totalen_chaos } }
}

doctrine_research_get_next_enabled_stage = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_next_stage = global.doctrine_research_current_stage }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_next_stage value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.gathering_storm compare = less_than }
			doctrine_research_evolution_i_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_next_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.gathering_storm }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_next_stage value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.rising_chaos compare = less_than }
			doctrine_research_evolution_ii_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_next_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.rising_chaos }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_next_stage value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.chaos_tier compare = less_than }
			doctrine_research_evolution_iii_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_next_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.chaos_tier }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_next_stage value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.totalen_chaos compare = less_than }
			doctrine_research_evolution_iv_enabled = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_next_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.totalen_chaos }
	}
}

doctrine_research_process_evolution = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_current_stage } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world } }
	set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_clock_started
	doctrine_research_get_highest_enabled_stage = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = doctrine_research_target_stage
				value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date } }
		set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date = global.date }
		add_to_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date = constant:doctrine_research_event.base_pacing_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = global.date
				value = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		doctrine_research_get_next_enabled_stage = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = doctrine_research_next_stage
					value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = doctrine_research_next_stage }
			clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date
			doctrine_research_record_new_evolutions = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = doctrine_research_target_stage
						value = global.doctrine_research_current_stage
						compare = greater_than
					}
				}
				set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date = global.date }
				add_to_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date = constant:doctrine_research_event.base_pacing_days }
			}
		}
		else = { clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date }
	}
}

doctrine_research_prepare_evolution_stage = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_current_stage } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world } }
	doctrine_research_get_highest_enabled_stage = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_clock_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_fired_once }
		}
		set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = doctrine_research_target_stage }
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_clock_started
		clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_evolution_date
	}
	else = { doctrine_research_process_evolution = yes }
}

doctrine_research_initialize_country_state = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_schema_version } }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_schema_version = 2 }
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_ids
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_stages
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_sizes
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_statuses
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_created_dates
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_human_started
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_army
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_navy
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_air
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_special_forces
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_chaos_warfare
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_ids
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_batch_ids
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_choice_numbers
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_states
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_actions
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_domains
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_grands
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_track_indices
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_subdoctrines
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_pre_levels
		clear_array = doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels
		clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_size = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_current_choice_number = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.none }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_receipt_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_batch_id = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_choice_number = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.none }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.invalid }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.invalid }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.invalid }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_concentrated = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_milestone_reached = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_current_level = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_next_level = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_maximum_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.max_mastery_level }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_completion = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected = 0 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_reached = 0 }
		doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_active_batch_index } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_active_batch_id } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_id = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_current_choice_number } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_current_choice_number = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_result } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_receipt_id } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_receipt_id = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_batch_id } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_batch_id = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_choice_number } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_choice_number = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_action } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.invalid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.invalid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_track } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.invalid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_summary_concentrated } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_concentrated = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_summary_milestone_reached } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_milestone_reached = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_current_level } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_current_level = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_next_level } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_next_level = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_maximum_level } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_maximum_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.max_mastery_level } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_completion } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_completion = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_display_milestone_reached } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_reached = 0 } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = doctrine_research_batch_human_started } }
		clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_human_started
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = doctrine_research_batch_ids }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
				end = doctrine_research_batch_ids^num
				value = doctrine_research_migration_batch_index
				set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_migration_human_started = 0 }
				if = { limit = { is_ai = no } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_migration_human_started = 1 } }
				add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_human_started value = doctrine_research_migration_human_started }
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { doctrine_research_country_state_is_aligned = yes } } set_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt
			doctrine_research_country_state_is_aligned = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt } }
		doctrine_research_validate_receipt_parent_links = yes
	}
}

# Country scope. Choice receipts are intentionally a separate append-only
# ledger: one batch owns one-to-five receipts, while a receipt belongs to one
# batch. Validate that foreign keys still point at a retained batch row without
# forcing the two ledgers to have the same cardinality.
doctrine_research_validate_receipt_parent_links = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_validation_failed = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = doctrine_research_receipt_ids }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
			end = doctrine_research_receipt_ids^num
			value = doctrine_research_receipt_parent_receipt_index
			set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_found = 0 }
			for_loop_effect = {
				start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
				end = doctrine_research_batch_ids^num
				value = doctrine_research_receipt_parent_batch_index
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_found < 1 }
						check_variable = {
							var = doctrine_research_receipt_batch_ids^doctrine_research_receipt_parent_receipt_index
							value = doctrine_research_batch_ids^doctrine_research_receipt_parent_batch_index
							compare = equals
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_found = 1 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_found < 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_validation_failed = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_parent_validation_failed > 0 } }
		set_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt
	}
}

doctrine_research_clear_selection = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_subdoctrine_page = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = constant:doctrine_research_domain.invalid }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.none }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.invalid }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.invalid }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
}

doctrine_research_prepare_display_levels = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_current_level = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_next_level = 1 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_maximum_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.max_mastery_level }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_completion = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_reached = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.peoples_war compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_maximum_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.peoples_war_max_mastery_level }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid compare = equals } }
		}
		doctrine_research_capture_transaction_level = yes
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_current_level = doctrine_research_transaction_observed_level }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_next_level = doctrine_research_display_current_level }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = doctrine_research_display_next_level
					value = doctrine_research_display_maximum_level
					compare = less_than
				}
			}
			add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_display_next_level = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = doctrine_research_display_current_level
					value = doctrine_research_display_maximum_level
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_completion = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { doctrine_research_selected_track_is_empty = yes }
				NOT = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine_is_active_incomplete = yes }
			}
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_completion = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = doctrine_research_display_next_level
				value = doctrine_research_display_maximum_level
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
			NOT = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_display_completion value = 1 compare = equals } }
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } has_completed_track = infantry }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals } has_completed_track = combat_support }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor compare = equals } has_completed_track = armor }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals } has_completed_track = operations }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.submarines compare = equals } has_completed_track = submarines }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.screens compare = equals } has_completed_track = screens }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.carriers compare = equals } has_completed_track = carriers }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.capital_ships compare = equals } has_completed_track = capital_ships }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.fighter_aircraft compare = equals } has_completed_track = fighter_aircraft }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.strike_aircraft compare = equals } has_completed_track = strike_aircraft }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.medium_aircraft compare = equals } has_completed_track = medium_aircraft }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.heavy_aircraft compare = equals } has_completed_track = heavy_aircraft }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.special_forces_first compare = equals } has_completed_track = special_forces_first }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.special_forces_second compare = equals } has_completed_track = special_forces_second }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } has_completed_track = infantry }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals } has_completed_track = combat_support }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor compare = equals } has_completed_track = armor }
				AND = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals } has_completed_track = operations }
			}
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_reached = 1 }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_milestone_expected = 0 }
	}
}

			NOT = { has_completed_subdoctrine = extermination_columns }
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.extermination_columns }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_display_found = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_display_found value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals }
			has_doctrine = contaminant_firebases
			NOT = { has_completed_subdoctrine = contaminant_firebases }
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.contaminant_firebases }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_display_found = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_display_found value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor compare = equals }
			has_doctrine = chemical_suppression
			NOT = { has_completed_subdoctrine = chemical_suppression }
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.chemical_suppression }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_display_found = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_display_found value = 0 compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_track value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals }
			has_doctrine = integrated_chemical_operations
			NOT = { has_completed_subdoctrine = integrated_chemical_operations }
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.integrated_chemical_operations }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_display_found = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_display_found value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
	}
	doctrine_research_prepare_display_levels = yes
}

doctrine_research_prepare_confirmation_display = {
	doctrine_research_restore_current_receipt_selection = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.adopt compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.invalid }
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_display_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine }
	}
	doctrine_research_prepare_display_levels = yes
}

doctrine_research_capture_selected_domain_grand_doctrine = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.invalid }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_mobile_warfare } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_mobile_warfare } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = superior_firepower } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.superior_firepower } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = grand_battleplan } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.grand_battleplan } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = mass_assault } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.mass_assault } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_fleet_in_being } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_fleet_in_being } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_convoy_raiding } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_convoy_raiding } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_base_strike } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_base_strike } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_strategic_destruction } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_strategic_destruction } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_battlefield_support } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_battlefield_support } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = new_operational_integrity } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.new_operational_integrity } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = special_forces_quantity } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quantity } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = special_forces_quality } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.special_forces_quality } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_domain value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found < 1 } has_doctrine = chaos_warfare } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = constant:doctrine_research_grand_doctrine.chaos_warfare } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_active_grand_found = 1 } }
	}
}

doctrine_research_prepare_firing_profile = {
	doctrine_research_prepare_evolution_stage = yes
	set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world }
	set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_stage = global.doctrine_research_current_stage }
	set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_size = constant:doctrine_research_event.base_batch_size }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.totalen_chaos } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_size = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iv_batch_size } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.chaos_tier } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_size = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iii_batch_size } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.rising_chaos } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_size = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_ii_batch_size } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { global.doctrine_research_current_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.gathering_storm } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_firing_size = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_i_batch_size } }
	set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_last_firing_size = global.doctrine_research_firing_size }
}

doctrine_research_record_new_evolutions = {
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_i_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.gathering_storm compare = greater_than_or_equals } NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_i_recorded } }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:doctrine_research_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_i_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_i_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_i_recorded
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_ii_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.rising_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals } NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_ii_recorded } }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:doctrine_research_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_ii_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_ii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_ii_recorded
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_iii_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.chaos_tier compare = greater_than_or_equals } NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_iii_recorded } }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:doctrine_research_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iii_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iii_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_iii_recorded
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_evolution_iv_enabled = yes check_variable = { var = global.doctrine_research_current_stage value = constant:doctrine_research_stage.totalen_chaos compare = greater_than_or_equals } NOT = { has_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_iv_recorded } }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_type = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_type }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_event_id = constant:doctrine_research_event.id }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_tier = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iv_tier }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_stage = constant:doctrine_research_event.evolution_iv_stage }
		set_temp_variable = { events_log_evolution_has_actor = 0 }
		record_events_log_evolution_entry = yes
		set_global_flag = doctrine_research_evolution_iv_recorded
	}
}

doctrine_research_fire_global_batch = {
	doctrine_research_initialize_adapter_registry = yes
	doctrine_research_prepare_firing_profile = yes
	doctrine_research_record_new_evolutions = yes
	every_country = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes }
		doctrine_research_append_country_batch = yes
	}
	set_global_flag = doctrine_research_fired_once
	clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_firing_stage
	clear_variable = global.doctrine_research_firing_size
}

doctrine_research_append_country_batch = {
	doctrine_research_initialize_country_state = yes
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_is_live = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_batch_id } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_batch_id = 0 } }
		add_to_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_batch_id = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_id = global.doctrine_research_next_batch_id }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_ids value = doctrine_research_new_batch_id }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_stages value = global.doctrine_research_firing_stage }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_sizes value = global.doctrine_research_firing_size }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices value = global.doctrine_research_firing_size }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_statuses value = constant:doctrine_research_batch_status.queued }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_created_dates value = global.date }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_human_started = 0 }
		if = { limit = { is_ai = no } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_human_started = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_human_started value = doctrine_research_new_batch_human_started }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_army = 0 }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_army_adoption_available = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_army = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_army value = doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_army }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_navy = 0 }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_navy_adoption_available = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_navy = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_navy value = doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_navy }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_air = 0 }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_air_adoption_available = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_air = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_air value = doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_air }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_special_forces = 0 }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_special_forces_adoption_available = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_special_forces = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_special_forces value = doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_special_forces }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_chaos_warfare = 0 }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_chaos_warfare_adoption_available = yes } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_chaos_warfare = 1 } }
		add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_batch_start_empty_chaos_warfare value = doctrine_research_new_batch_empty_chaos_warfare }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch } } doctrine_research_start_next_batch = yes }
	}
}

doctrine_research_start_next_batch = {
	doctrine_research_initialize_country_state = yes
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch } NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt } doctrine_research_country_is_live = yes }
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
			end = doctrine_research_batch_ids^num
			value = doctrine_research_batch_scan_index
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index < 0 } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_statuses^doctrine_research_batch_scan_index value = constant:doctrine_research_batch_status.queued compare = equals } }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index = doctrine_research_batch_scan_index }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_batch_statuses^doctrine_research_batch_scan_index = constant:doctrine_research_batch_status.active }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index > constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index } }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_id = doctrine_research_batch_ids^doctrine_research_active_batch_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_stage = doctrine_research_batch_stages^doctrine_research_active_batch_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_size = doctrine_research_batch_sizes^doctrine_research_active_batch_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_current_choice_number = 1 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions = 0 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery = 0 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 0 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 0 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_concentrated = 0 }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_milestone_reached = 0 }
			set_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
			doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
				doctrine_research_ai_resolve_active_batch = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
				if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.2 } }
				else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.11 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

doctrine_research_find_current_receipt = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_found = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
		end = doctrine_research_receipt_ids^num
		value = doctrine_research_receipt_scan_index
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_found < 1 } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_batch_ids^doctrine_research_receipt_scan_index value = doctrine_research_active_batch_id compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_choice_numbers^doctrine_research_receipt_scan_index value = doctrine_research_current_choice_number compare = equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_found = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = doctrine_research_receipt_scan_index }
		}
	}
}

doctrine_research_restore_current_receipt_selection = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_found = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending }
		doctrine_research_find_current_receipt = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_found value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = doctrine_research_receipt_actions^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_grands^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_subdoctrines^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
		}
	}
}

doctrine_research_capture_track_index = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_track_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_track_index = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_track_index = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_track_index = 2 } }

doctrine_research_prepare_receipt = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = global.doctrine_research_next_receipt_id } } set_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_receipt_id = 0 } }
	add_to_variable = { global.doctrine_research_next_receipt_id = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_new_id = global.doctrine_research_next_receipt_id }
	doctrine_research_capture_track_index = yes
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_ids value = doctrine_research_receipt_new_id }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_batch_ids value = doctrine_research_active_batch_id }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_choice_numbers value = doctrine_research_current_choice_number }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_states value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.prepared }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_actions value = doctrine_research_selected_action }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_domains value = doctrine_research_selected_domain }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_grands value = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks value = doctrine_research_selected_track }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_track_indices value = doctrine_research_transaction_track_index }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_subdoctrines value = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_pre_levels value = doctrine_research_transaction_pre_level }
	add_to_array = { array = doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels value = 0 }
	set_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = doctrine_research_receipt_ids^num }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = 1 }
}
