	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_grands^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_track = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_subdoctrines^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level = doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
}

doctrine_research_record_invalid_transaction = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.invalid }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_receipt_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_batch_id = doctrine_research_active_batch_id }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_choice_number = doctrine_research_current_choice_number }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_action = doctrine_research_selected_action }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain = doctrine_research_selected_domain }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_track = doctrine_research_selected_track }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level = 0 }
}

doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.ambiguous }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_receipt_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_batch_id = doctrine_research_active_batch_id }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_choice_number = doctrine_research_current_choice_number }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_action = doctrine_research_selected_action }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_domain = doctrine_research_selected_domain }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_grand_doctrine = doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_track = doctrine_research_selected_track }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_post_level = 0 }
}

doctrine_research_finalize_receipt = {
	clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.effect_applied }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels^doctrine_research_receipt_index = doctrine_research_transaction_post_level }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_remaining_choices = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_new_remaining_choices = 1 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index = doctrine_research_new_remaining_choices }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.choice_consumed }
	add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_current_choice_number = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_success = 1 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.success }
	doctrine_research_record_last_transaction = yes
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.adopt } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery = 1 } }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_milestone_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.max_mastery_level }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine value = constant:doctrine_research_subdoctrine.peoples_war compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_milestone_level = constant:doctrine_research_event.peoples_war_max_mastery_level }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = doctrine_research_transaction_post_level
				value = doctrine_research_transaction_milestone_level
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_milestone_reached = 1 }
	}
	doctrine_research_update_achievements = yes
}

doctrine_research_close_active_batch = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_close_index = doctrine_research_active_batch_index }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_close_index > constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_batch_close_as_quarantine } set_variable = { doctrine_research_batch_statuses^doctrine_research_close_index = constant:doctrine_research_batch_status.quarantined } }
		else = { set_variable = { doctrine_research_batch_statuses^doctrine_research_close_index = constant:doctrine_research_batch_status.completed } }
	}
	clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
	clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
	clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_batch_close_as_quarantine
	clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_id = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_stage = constant:doctrine_research_stage.baseline_calm_world }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_active_batch_size = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_current_choice_number = 1 }
	doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
	doctrine_research_update_achievements = yes
}

doctrine_research_close_active_batch_and_start_next = {
	doctrine_research_close_active_batch = yes
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_is_live = yes } doctrine_research_start_next_batch = yes }
}

doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = {
	set_country_flag = doctrine_research_batch_close_as_quarantine
	set_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_failed
	doctrine_research_close_active_batch = yes
}

doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch_and_start_next = {
	doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_is_live = yes } doctrine_research_start_next_batch = yes }
}

doctrine_research_confirm_selection = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_success = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_post_level = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_native_completed = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_proof = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_found = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_event.invalid_index }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.invalid }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
		}
		doctrine_research_find_current_receipt = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery compare = equals }
		}
		doctrine_research_capture_selected_domain_grand_doctrine = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_found value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = yes
		doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch
			check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending }
					doctrine_research_selected_choice_is_valid = yes
				}
				check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_found value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_found value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = doctrine_research_receipt_actions^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_domain = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_grands^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_track = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_subdoctrine = doctrine_research_receipt_subdoctrines^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.choice_consumed compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
				set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_success = 1 }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.success }
				doctrine_research_record_last_transaction = yes
				doctrine_research_update_achievements = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.effect_applied compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery compare = equals } }
					# An effect-applied receipt is recoverable only when the stored and
					# current native levels both prove exactly pre-level + one step.
					# Re-read even when a positive post-level was stored so external
					# mastery changes cannot turn an interrupted receipt into a free
					# choice consumption.
					set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_expected = doctrine_research_receipt_pre_levels^doctrine_research_receipt_index }
					add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_expected = constant:doctrine_research_event.mastery_point_increment }
					doctrine_research_capture_transaction_level = yes
					if = {
						limit = {
							doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_active = yes
							check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels^doctrine_research_receipt_index value = doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_expected compare = equals }
							check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_transaction_observed_level value = doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_expected compare = equals }
						}
						set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_proof = 1 }
						set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_post_level = doctrine_research_transaction_observed_level }
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.adopt compare = equals }
						doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_active = yes
					}
					set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_proof = 1 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_recovery_proof value = 0 compare = greater_than }
						}
					}
					doctrine_research_finalize_receipt = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.ambiguous }
					doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = yes
					doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.native_adoption compare = equals } }
				if = {
					limit = { doctrine_research_selected_grand_doctrine_is_active = yes }
					clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
					set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.adoption_only }
					doctrine_research_record_last_transaction = yes
					doctrine_research_update_achievements = yes
				}
				else = {
					set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.ambiguous }
					doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = yes
					doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.invalid compare = equals } }
				clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
				doctrine_research_record_invalid_transaction = yes
			}
			else = {
				# Prepared, ambiguous, and abandoned receipts have no durable proof that
				# the native effect did not already happen. Fail closed and quarantine.
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.ambiguous }
				doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = yes
				doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery compare = equals } }
				doctrine_research_capture_transaction_level = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_pre_level = 0 }
			}
			doctrine_research_prepare_receipt = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.adopt compare = equals } }
				doctrine_research_apply_adoption = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery compare = equals }
					doctrine_research_selected_track_is_empty = yes
				}
				doctrine_research_apply_empty_track_mastery = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_selected_action value = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery compare = equals } }
				doctrine_research_apply_mastery = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_transaction_success value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.effect_applied }
				doctrine_research_finalize_receipt = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_transaction_native_completed value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				doctrine_research_capture_transaction_level = yes
				set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_post_level = doctrine_research_transaction_observed_level }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.native_adoption }
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_post_levels^doctrine_research_receipt_index = doctrine_research_transaction_post_level }
				clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_transaction_receipt_pending
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.adoption_only }
				doctrine_research_record_last_transaction = yes
				doctrine_research_update_achievements = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_receipt_index = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.ambiguous }
				doctrine_research_record_ambiguous_transaction = yes
				doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		doctrine_research_record_invalid_transaction = yes
	}
}

doctrine_research_continue_after_success = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.3 } }
		else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.11 } }
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.10 } }
}

doctrine_research_prepare_batch_summary = {
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 0 }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_receipt_count = doctrine_research_receipt_ids^num }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_army_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_navy_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_air_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_special_forces_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_chaos_warfare_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_infantry_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_combat_support_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_armor_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_operations_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_submarines_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_screens_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_carriers_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_capital_ships_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_fighter_aircraft_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_strike_aircraft_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_medium_aircraft_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_heavy_aircraft_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_first_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_second_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_infantry_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_combat_support_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_armor_seen = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_operations_seen = 0 }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = constant:doctrine_research_event.first_index
		end = doctrine_research_summary_receipt_count
		value = doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_batch_ids^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = doctrine_research_active_batch_id compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_states^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_receipt_state.choice_consumed compare = equals }
			}
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_army_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_army_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_navy_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_navy_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_air_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_air_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_special_forces_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_special_forces_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_chaos_warfare_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_chaos_warfare_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_infantry_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_infantry_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_combat_support_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_combat_support_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.armor compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_armor_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_armor_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.army compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_operations_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_operations_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.submarines compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_submarines_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_submarines_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.screens compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_screens_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_screens_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.carriers compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_carriers_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_carriers_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.navy compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.capital_ships compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_capital_ships_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_capital_ships_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.fighter_aircraft compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_fighter_aircraft_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_fighter_aircraft_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.strike_aircraft compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_strike_aircraft_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_strike_aircraft_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.medium_aircraft compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_medium_aircraft_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_medium_aircraft_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.air compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.heavy_aircraft compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_heavy_aircraft_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_heavy_aircraft_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.special_forces_first compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_first_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_first_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.special_forces compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.special_forces_second compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_second_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_second_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.infantry compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_infantry_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_infantry_seen = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.combat_support compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_combat_support_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_combat_support_seen = 1 } }

			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_domains^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_domain.chaos_warfare compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_receipt_tracks^doctrine_research_summary_receipt_index value = constant:doctrine_research_track.operations compare = equals } check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_operations_seen < 1 } } set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_operations_seen = 1 } }
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_army_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_navy_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_air_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_special_forces_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_domain_chaos_warfare_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_domains = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_infantry_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_combat_support_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_armor_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_army_operations_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_submarines_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_screens_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_carriers_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_navy_capital_ships_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_fighter_aircraft_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_strike_aircraft_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_medium_aircraft_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_air_heavy_aircraft_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_first_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_special_forces_special_forces_second_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_infantry_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_combat_support_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_armor_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_track_chaos_warfare_operations_seen > 0 } } add_to_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks = 1 } }
	set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_concentrated = 0 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_summary_distinct_tracks value = 1 compare = less_than_or_equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_adoptions value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_active_batch_successful_mastery value = 0 compare = greater_than } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_summary_concentrated = 1 } }
}

doctrine_research_complete_active_batch = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch } doctrine_research_close_active_batch_and_start_next = yes } }

# Country scope. Lifecycle callbacks call this only for the exact country
# affected by the engine callback. The ledger remains country-owned across
# controller, subject, government, and faction changes; only the unresolved
# presentation or AI resolver is resumed when a queued or active batch exists.
doctrine_research_reconcile_country_lifecycle = {
	doctrine_research_initialize_country_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			doctrine_research_country_is_live = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_state_corrupt }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch }
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes }
				clr_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
				doctrine_research_ai_resolve_active_batch = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_result value = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.adoption_only compare = equals } }
				set_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
				country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.13 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human } }
				set_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch_prompted_human
				if = { limit = { doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes } country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.2 } }
				else = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr27.11 } }
			}
		}
		else = { doctrine_research_start_next_batch = yes }
	}
}

doctrine_research_ai_score_domains = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = 0 }
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_army_has_valid_action = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { num_divisions > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_template_containing_unit = infantry has_template_containing_unit = motorized has_template_containing_unit = light_armor has_template_containing_unit = medium_armor has_template_containing_unit = heavy_armor } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_army_track_infantry_action_available = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_army_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_army = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.owner_readiness }
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_navy_has_valid_action = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_navy_size = { unit = submarine size > 0 } has_navy_size = { unit = destroyer size > 0 } OR = { has_navy_size = { unit = light_cruiser size > 0 } has_navy_size = { unit = heavy_cruiser size > 0 } } has_navy_size = { unit = battleship size > 0 } has_navy_size = { unit = carrier size > 0 } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_naval_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_navy_track_submarines_action_available = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_navy_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_navy_owner_system_ready = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_navy = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.owner_readiness } }
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_air_has_valid_action = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_deployed_air_force_size = { type = fighter size > 0 } has_deployed_air_force_size = { type = cas size > 0 } has_deployed_air_force_size = { type = naval_bomber size > 0 } has_deployed_air_force_size = { type = tactical_bomber size > 0 } has_deployed_air_force_size = { type = strategic_bomber size > 0 } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }
		if = { limit = { num_of_military_factories > 0 } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.production } }
		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_air_track_fighter_aircraft_action_available = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_air_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_air_owner_system_ready = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_air = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.owner_readiness } }
	}
	if = { limit = { doctrine_research_special_forces_has_valid_action = yes }
		add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.base }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_template_containing_unit = mountaineers has_template_containing_unit = marine has_template_containing_unit = paratrooper has_template_containing_unit = ranger_battalion } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.forces } }

		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { any_controlled_state = { is_coastal = yes } any_controlled_state = { has_terrain = mountains } } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.geography } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_special_forces = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }

		if = { limit = { has_war = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.war } }

		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.theater } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_chaos_warfare_track_infantry_action_available = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.completion } }
		if = { limit = { doctrine_research_ai_has_chaos_warfare_strategy_signal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.strategy } }
		if = { limit = { cbrn_chaos_warfare_ai_has_viable_program = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_score_chaos_warfare = constant:doctrine_research_ai_weight.owner_readiness } }
	}

doctrine_research_ai_pick_choice = {
	doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
	doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_domain = yes
	if = {
		limit = { doctrine_research_selected_domain_adoption_available = yes }
		doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_grand = yes
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_grand_found > 0 } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.adopt } }
	}
	else = {
		doctrine_research_capture_selected_domain_grand_doctrine = yes
		doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_track = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_track_found > 0 } }
			doctrine_research_ai_choose_scored_subdoctrine = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_subdoctrine_found > 0 } } set_variable = { doctrine_research_selected_action = constant:doctrine_research_action.mastery } }
		}
	}
}

doctrine_research_ai_resolve_active_batch = {
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_choice_guard = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_batch_id = doctrine_research_active_batch_id }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_active_batch_id value = doctrine_research_ai_batch_id compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_choice_guard < constant:doctrine_research_event.max_batch_choices } doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes }
	doctrine_research_ai_pick_choice = yes
		if = {
			limit = { doctrine_research_selected_choice_is_valid = yes }
			doctrine_research_confirm_selection = yes
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { doctrine_research_transaction_success > 0 } } add_to_temp_variable = { doctrine_research_ai_choice_guard = 1 } }
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_last_transaction_result value = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.adoption_only compare = equals } }
				doctrine_research_clear_selection = yes
				set_variable = { doctrine_research_last_transaction_result = constant:doctrine_research_transaction_result.none }
			}
			else = { doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch_and_start_next = yes }
		}
		else = { doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch_and_start_next = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = doctrine_research_active_batch check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_active_batch_id value = doctrine_research_ai_batch_id compare = equals } check_variable = { var = doctrine_research_batch_remaining_choices^doctrine_research_active_batch_index value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { doctrine_research_country_has_valid_action = yes } } doctrine_research_complete_active_batch = yes }
		else = { doctrine_research_quarantine_active_batch_and_start_next = yes }
	}
}

doctrine_research_discard_annexed_country_state = {
	clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_ids
	clear_array = doctrine_research_batch_stages
